 CONSULAR LIST

DEPARTMENT OF PROTOCOL 
 
MINISTRY OF FOREIGN AFFAIRS 
 
 BANGKOK 
  
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
Any amendment of the information contained in this online publication shall be 
submitted to the Protocol Division, Ministry of Foreign Affairs via Email address: 
protocol02@mfa.go.th 
 
The information contained in this online publication list is up-to-date at the time 
of editing. Kindly note that, if deemed appropriate, information appears in this 
online publication is subject to frequent amendments. 
 
The information on  
(a) “Order of Precedence” is as of 30 March 2026 
(b) “Diplomatic Missions” is as received on 10 April 2026  
(c) “Consular Representatives” and “Consular Representatives” (Honorary)       
  is as of 10 March 2026 
(d) “United Nations Organizations and International Organizations” 
  is as received on 18 March 2026 
 
Spelling of districts, sub-districts, and provinces is in accordance with the Office  
of the Royal Society’s criterion.

PUBLIC HOLIDAYS FOR 2026 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
 
   
PUBLIC HOLIDAYS FOR 2026 
*   *   *   *   * 
January   Thursday 1 January  New Year’s Day 
 Friday 2 January  Additional Public Holiday on the Occasion of New Year 
February --  -- -- 
March Tuesday 3 March Makha Bucha Day 
April Monday 6 April  Chakri Memorial Day  
 Monday 13 April Songkran Festival Day 
 Tuesday 14 April Songkran Festival Day 
 Wednesday 15 April  Songkran Festival Day 
May Monday 4 May Coronation Day 
 Sunday 31 May Visakha Bucha Day 
June Monday 1 June Substitution for Visakha Bucha Day 
  Wednesday 3 June Her Majesty Queen Suthida Bajrasudhabimalalakshana’s  
     Birthday Anniversary  
July Tuesday 28 July His Majesty King Maha Vajiralongkorn  
    Phra Vajiraklaochaoyuhua’s Birthday Anniversary 
 Wednesday 29 July Asarnha Bucha Day 
 Thursday 30 July Buddhist Lent Day 
August Wednesday 12 August Birthday Anniversary of Her Majesty Queen Sirikit The Queen Mother’s  
    and Thailand’s Mother’s Day 
September --  -- -- 
October Tuesday 13 October His Majesty King Bhumibol Adulyadej The Great Memorial Day  
 Friday 23 October King Chulalongkorn Memorial Day 
November --  -- -- 
December Saturday 5 December Birthday Anniversary of His Majesty King Bhumibol Adulyadej The Great,  
     National Day, and Thailand’s Father’s Day  
 Monday 7 December Substitution for Birthday Anniversary of His Majesty King Bhumibol  
    Adulyadej The Great, National Day, and Thailand’s Father’s Day 
 Thursday 10 December  Constitution Day 
 Thursday 31 December   New Year’s Eve  
January 2027 Friday 1 January     New Year’s Day 2027 
Note        - If an official holiday falls on Saturday or Sunday, the next working day will be declared                      
           a substituted public holiday
